The RISC-V machine learning ecosystem is evaluated, from instruction set extensions and core implementations to compiler optimizations and deployment strategies. Key contributions include a unified taxonomy of RISC-V ML implementations, a comparative analysis of performance and design trade-offs, an evaluation of software toolchain maturity, and the identification of emerging trends in instruction set extensions and specialized accelerators. Findings reveal progress in energy efficiency, specialized instruction development, and framework integration, while highlighting challenges in standardization, verification complexity, and ecosystem fragmentation. The analysis proposes four research directions to address current limitations: specialized neural processing extensions, adaptive and modular processor architectures, security frameworks, and energy-efficient multi-domain architectures. These directions provide a roadmap for advancing RISC-V as a foundational platform for next-generation machine learning systems.

</details>


### [107] [Don't Mask the Environment: Observation Supervision Changes How Agents Explore Under RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20715)
*Juzheng Zhang,Disha Makhija,Manoj Ghuhan Arivazhagan,Vinayshekhar Bannihatti Kumar,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ActObs，在 SFT 阶段同时监督轨迹中已有的观测 token，不增加数据、参数、序列长度或前向次数，从而提升后续 RL 的探索能力与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 SFT 只对智能体生成的 action token 计算损失，将环境 observation 仅作为上下文而不作为预测目标。作者质疑这一惯例是否为后续强化学习提供了最佳初始化。

Method: ActObs 在 SFT 中额外监督轨迹中已存在的 observation token。尽管部署时智能体不生成观测，预测观测可促使策略建模动作后果，且不引入额外数据、参数、序列 token 或前向计算。随后进行 GRPO 强化学习并分析梯度动态。

Result: SFT 后两法表现相近，GRPO 后出现分化：Qwen3-4B 上，ActObs 在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 各采样预算下 pass@k 均更高；Qwen3-8B 上以部分 pass@1 换取更高 pass@k（pass@16 提升 3.4 pp）并解决更多不同任务；跨域代码编辑 aider-polyglot 上 4B 模型 pass@1 提升 4.2 pp（SFT 与 RL 均未见该任务）。ActObs 在 RL 中保留更多熵、所需策略移动更少，最终策略更接近 SFT 初始化。

Conclusion: 分析将差异追溯到 SFT：action 与 observation 梯度迅速正交，仅用 action 训练会留下较大的观测梯度残差，并使环境预测能力退化至低于基座模型。联合监督可防止这种单向特化，保持后果预测能力，为下游探索做好准备。

Abstract: Agent trajectories record what an agent does and what happens next. Yet standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies loss only to agent-authored action tokens, using environment observations as context but not as prediction targets. We ask whether this convention provides the best initialization for subsequent reinforcement learning. We introduce ActObs, which also supervises the observation tokens already present in each trajectory. Although deployed agents never generate observations, learning to predict them encourages the policy to model action consequences without adding data, parameters, sequence tokens, or forward passes. The methods perform similarly after SFT but diverge after GRPO. On Qwen3-4B, GRPO from ActObs achieves higher pass@k at every evaluated sampling budget than its action-only counterpart on Terminal-Bench 2.0. On Qwen3-8B, it trades some pass@1 reliability for higher pass@k (+3.4 pp at pass@16) and solves more distinct tasks. The advantage extends to cross-domain code editing on aider-polyglot (+4.2 pp at pass@1 at 4B), whose tasks are unseen during SFT and RL. ActObs retains more entropy during RL while requiring less policy movement, leaving the final policy closer to its SFT initialization. Our analysis traces this difference to SFT: action and observation gradients rapidly become orthogonal, while action-only training leaves a large residual observation gradient and degrades environment prediction below the base model. Joint supervision prevents this one-sided specialization, preserving consequence prediction and preparing the policy for downstream exploration.

</details>


### [108] [Video DeltaNet: A Video-Native Hybrid Attention for Livestream Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20744)
*Haocheng Xi,Yiming Xie,Hexu Zhao,Yiwen Zhang,Michael Liu,Thomas Creavin,Kurt Keutzer,Xiuyu Li,Zhaoyang Lv,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Video DeltaNet (VDN)，以局部 Softmax 注意力结合双向线性记忆建模长程视频上下文，其中视频 Delta 注意力 (VDA) 每帧以空间 token 联合更新一次记忆；在 MiniMax H3 上实例化为 VDN-H3，配合 8 步蒸馏与 SGLang 优化，实现 14.3 秒、768p 视频 6.70 秒完成 DiT 去噪，较 50 步稠密基线加速 14.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在去噪过程中需反复处理长时空 token 序列，注意力机制成为主要计算瓶颈。线性注意力虽在大语言模型中广泛应用，但直接迁移到视频模型往往无法保留高质量生成所需的细粒度交互。

Method: VDN 将局部 Softmax 注意力与双向线性记忆结合以捕获长程视频上下文。其线性分支引入 Video Delta Attention (VDA)，通过联合纳入每帧的空间 token 实现每帧一次的记忆更新；两支路使用独立的输出投影与可学习门控进行校准，并采用分阶段教师对齐策略将新路径逐步注入预训练模型。在 MiniMax H3 上实例化时，混合方案用于视频—视频交互，涉及文本或音频的交互仍保留 Softmax。此外使用八步蒸馏与优化的 SGLang 推理栈。

Result: VDN-H3 在八块 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上以 6.70 秒完成 14.3 秒、768p 视频的 DiT 去噪，在相同 GPU 数量下相较 50 步稠密 H3 基线取得 14.5 倍加速。

Conclusion: 混合局部 Softmax 与双向线性 Delta 记忆的注意力设计，结合分阶段教师对齐、步数蒸馏与推理栈优化，可在保持生成质量所需细粒度交互的同时显著降低视频扩散模型的计算开销，验证了线性记忆路径在视频生成中的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Video diffusion models repeatedly process long spatiotemporal token sequences during denoising, making attention a major computational bottleneck. Linear attention offers an appealing alternative and has been widely adopted in recent large language models, but directly applying it to video models often fails to preserve the fine-grained interactions required for high-quality generation. We present Video DeltaNet (VDN), which combines local Softmax attention with bidirectional linear memory for long-range video context. Its linear branch introduces Video Delta Attention (VDA), which updates memory once per frame by jointly incorporating its spatial tokens. Separate output projections and learnable gates calibrate the two branches, while a staged teacher-alignment recipe progressively introduces the new pathway into pretrained models. We instantiate VDN on MiniMax H3, applying the hybrid to video-to-video interactions while retaining Softmax for interactions involving text or audio. With eight-step distillation and an optimized SGLang serving stack, VDN-H3 completes DiT denoising for a 14.3-second, 768p video in 6.70 seconds on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs, corresponding to a 14.5x speedup over the 50-step dense H3 baseline on the same GPU count.

</details>


### [109] [Calibrated RF-Fingerprinting Under Interference With Heterogeneous Transmission Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20765)
*Tariq Abdul-Quddoos,Xiangfang Li,Lijun Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究共信道干扰下的射频指纹识别，将其建模为多标签分类问题，采用1D CNN并用保假阴性上界的校准方法，在POWDER 5G testbed实测数据上准确率达73%–97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RF指纹研究几乎只考虑单一发射机场景，忽略多信号在时频上重叠的共信道干扰，限制了实际适用性；本文旨在推进该场景下的研究。

Method: 将问题形式化为多标签分类，采用1D卷积神经网络（CNN）；对模型进行校准以推导标签概率的置信阈值，并给出平均假阴性数量上界的保证。

Result: 基于POWDER 5G测试床实测数据（802.11a Wi-Fi、4G LTE、5G NR波形）验证：校准后准确率依信道条件从73%到97%不等；针对不同平均假阴性上界校准可达到约(1−校准假阴性)的micro recall，且校准对分布外干扰具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在高竞争的真实无线环境中具有应用潜力，能兼顾不遗漏真实频谱政策违规的置信度保证。

Abstract: Radio Frequency(RF)-Fingerprinting is a spectrum monitoring technique that identifies specific transmitters based on hardware impairments imprinted within the emitted signal. Although widely researched, studies almost exclusively consider scenarios where only one transmitter is emitting at a time, limiting real world applicability. In this work, we further the study of RF-Fingerprinting by considering co-channel interference, with multiple emitted signals interfering with each other, overlapping in time and frequency. Specifically, we formulate this problem as a multi-label classification problem and employ a 1D convolutional neural network (CNN). Furthermore, the models are calibrated such that the confidence thresholds for the label probabilities are derived, with guarantees on the upper bound on the average number of False Negatives, providing a degree of confidence in not missing a true spectrum policy violation. The proposed method is validated using real world data from the POWDER 5G testbed on devices transmitting 802.11a(Wi-Fi), 4G LTE, and 5G NR waveforms. The results show accuracy as high as 97% and as low as 73% after calibration depending on channel conditions. Also calibrating for various average false negatives upper bounds achieves micro recall scores of approximately (1 - calibrated false negatives) with the calibration robust to out-of-distribution interference, demonstrating the potential of the proposed method in a realistic high contention wireless environment

</details>


### [110] [PosteriorBench: From Point Estimates to Posterior Matching in Evaluating Generative Inverse Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20794)
*Jiachen Yao,Zi-Siang Hsu,Xi Deng,Aditi Gupta,Xin Ju,Sally M Benson,Gege Wen,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PosteriorBench，一个用于评估生成式逆问题求解器分布精度的基准，涵盖四个物理逆问题，并配套五项后验评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式逆问题评估主要关注能否生成单一合理重建，但对病态问题而言，同一稀疏或含噪观测可能对应多个解。此时方法可能在点估计精度上表现优异，却因模式坍缩、不确定性过度自信或对不兼容解取平均，而无法刻画真实后验分布。

Method: 构建高保真参考后验，采用拒绝采样与马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛等计算密集但成熟的方法；覆盖Darcy流反演、Poisson源恢复、碳捕集与封存、光传输材料推断四类物理逆问题；设计五项指标——后验均值误差、后验标准差误差、最大均值差异、切片Wasserstein距离、径向平均功率谱误差，并统一分布匹配与不确定性量化流程。

Result: 实验揭示当前求解器普遍存在显著分布匹配差距；神经算子提升了分辨率鲁棒性；引导权重与生成噪声是后验方差校准的关键因素。

Conclusion: PosteriorBench为生成式逆求解器提供了从点精度转向分布精度的系统评估框架，涵盖稀疏感知、低分辨率观测、非线性前向模型、不同噪声水平及多模态先验等场景。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to solve scientific inverse problems, but existing evaluations still focus primarily on whether a method can produce a single plausible reconstruction. This is insufficient for ill-posed problems, where multiple solutions may be consistent with the same sparse or noisy observations. In these settings, a method can achieve strong pointwise accuracy while still failing to capture the true posterior through mode collapse, overconfident uncertainty, or averaging incompatible solutions. We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for evaluating the distributional accuracy of generative inverse solvers. PosteriorBench evaluates four physics-based inverse problems: Darcy flow inversion, Poisson source recovery, carbon capture and storage, and light transport material inference. For each task, we construct high-fidelity reference posteriors using computationally heavy but established procedures such as rejection sampling and Markov chain Monte Carlo, enabling direct assessment of whether solvers recover the full set of solutions rather than the single best sample. We pair these references with a five-metric posterior evaluation suite: posterior-mean error, posterior-standard-deviation error, maximum mean discrepancy, sliced Wasserstein distance, and radially averaged power-spectrum error. These metrics assess pointwise accuracy, marginal uncertainty, distributional alignment, and global frequency fidelity. The benchmark spans sparse sensing, low-resolution observations, nonlinear forward models, varying noise levels, and multimodal priors, with a unified pipeline for distribution matching and uncertainty quantification. Our experiments reveal substantial distribution-matching gaps across current solvers, while showing that neural operators improve resolution robustness, and guidance weights and generation noise are key to posterior-variance calibration.

</details>


### [111] [Score Centering Stabilizes Off-policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20807)
*Martin Marek,Max Ryabinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示大语言模型强化学习中训练-推理不匹配（TIM）导致的不稳定性主要源自"漂移"（drift）——训练与推理引擎间随训练步累积的持续性偏差，并提出一种加性的"score centering"校正项以抵消漂移，从而稳定RL训练，其效果优于或媲美重要性采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习对训练引擎与推理引擎之间的微小差异（训练-推理不匹配，TIM）极为敏感，而完全消除TIM会严重损害rollout效率，因此不切实际。作者旨在找出TIM下RL不稳定的根本原因并提供高效的解决方案。

Method: 作者将TIM下RL的不稳定性归因于"漂移"（drift），即训练与推理引擎之间随每个训练步累积的持续性偏差；在此基础上推导出一个加性的"score centering"校正项，通过抵消漂移来稳定RL训练。由于该校正项为加性形式，它还可以与重要性采样方法组合使用。

Result: 在0.6B到30B参数规模的模型上训练时，仅使用score centering即可在量化条件下达到或超过基于重要性采样的方法，且当不匹配越严重时差距越大。由于其加性特性，score centering还能与重要性采样组合，其组合方法在陈旧性（staleness）实验中优于纯重要性采样基线。

Conclusion: TIM下RL的不稳定根源在于随训练步累积的漂移偏差；通过引入加性的score centering校正项可有效抵消漂移，稳定训练。该方法在多种模型规模下表现优异且可扩展，并能与重要性采样协同，为应对训练-推理不匹配提供了一种高效且通用的解决思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models is notoriously sensitive to small differences between training and inference engines, often referred to as the training-inference mismatch (TIM). However, completely eliminating TIM is impractical, as it would come at a major cost to rollout efficiency. In this paper, we show that the instability of RL under TIM is primarily caused by drift: a persistent bias between training and inference engines that accumulates with every training step. We derive an additive "score centering" correction term that stabilizes RL under TIM by canceling drift. When training models from 0.6B to 30B parameters, score centering alone matches or outperforms methods based on importance sampling under quantization, with the gap growing as the mismatch becomes more severe. Because the correction is additive, score centering also composes with importance sampling -- their composition outperforms pure importance-sampling baselines in our staleness experiments.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [The Motile-Units model: Interacting spins model of cell polarization and motility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20587)
*Jonathan E. Ron,Nir S. Gov*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种二维细胞运动的粗粒化相互作用自旋模型，将细胞周长离散为随机二元自旋，由肌动蛋白逆流驱动的极性线索产生长程相互作用，展现三种动力学相，并自然复现趋化、光遗传转向与竞争刺激下的方向决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统活性粒子模型通常将速度与极性作为粒子层面的预设变量，难以从细胞内部机制自然涌现，因此需要一种更底层的、由内部随机动力学驱动运动与极性的建模框架来研究细胞迁移。

Method: 将细胞周长离散为在活跃/非活跃态间切换的随机二元自旋，每个自旋代表一个"运动单元"，活跃时产生突出力与逆流；通过被集体肌动蛋白逆流平流的极性线索实现运动单元间的长程相互作用；并加入最近邻相互作用进行调制。

Result: 模型呈现三种动力学相：随机行走相、持续随机行走相，以及以"跑-停"迁移为特征的间歇双稳相。最近邻相互作用可调节速度与持续性但不改变整体相图。框架还可复现趋化迁移、局域光遗传激活转向以及竞争刺激下的方向决策（对称破缺）。

Conclusion: 该模型引入了一类新型活性粒子模型，其速度与极性均由内部随机自旋动力学涌现而非作为粒子级变量强加，为细胞迁移研究提供框架并拓展了活性物质物理的范围。

Abstract: We introduce a coarse-grained interacting-spin model for two-dimensional cell motility, in which the cell perimeter is discretized into stochastic binary spins that switch between active and inactive states. Each perimeter spin represents a "motile-unit" that is a source of protrusive force and retrograde flow when active. Long-range interactions between the motile-units arise through a polarity cue advected by the collective actin retrograde flow, providing a minimal realization of spontaneous symmetry breaking and self-propulsion. The model exhibits three dynamical phases, a random walk phase, persistent random walk phase, and an intermittent bistable phase characterized by run-and-tumble migration. Additional nearest-neighbor interactions modulate speed and persistence without altering the overall phase structure. Owing to its simplicity, the framework naturally incorporates external cues, reproducing chemotactic migration, steering by localized optogenetic activation, and directional decision-making (symmetry breaking) under competing stimuli. The model introduces a new class of active-particle model in which both speed and polarity emerge from internal stochastic spin dynamics, rather than being imposed as particle-level variables, offering a framework for the study of cell migration and extends the scope of active-matter physics.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [A Mathematical Model of Motivated Emotional Mind - Cognitive Embodied System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20437)
*Wiesław L. Galus,Janusz A. Starzyk*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文为具身智能系统提出"动机情绪心智"认知架构的数学模型，核心是形式化再入循环与表征选择机制，并将动机学习（ML）刻画为基于需求阈值、情感调控的广义强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习模型难以充分解释受需求、情感与当前调节状态驱动的具身系统在调节压力下的反应选择，因此需要一个更严谨的认知现象形式化框架，为理论分析、仿真与AI实现奠定基础。

Method: 构建数学模型，形式化整合前馈加工、侧向交互与反馈通路的再入循环及表征选择机制；规定外感受与内感受信号、身体-动机情境与记忆痕迹如何绑定为联想记忆结构"semblions"并竞争进一步加工与自上而下重构；涵盖次级知觉、表征竞争、好奇心、程序性缺口与指向限制稳态失衡的动作选择；引入需求阈值、目标生成与目标转移、身体状态、资源约束与动作不确定性。

Result: 提出一个将动机学习定制于具身系统（其动力学由需求、情感和当前调节状态塑造）的形式化框架；全局情感作为中心控制信号，调节学习率、表征效价以及探索-利用平衡。

Conclusion: 该模型是迈向认知现象更严谨形式化的一步，可为后续理论分析、计算机仿真及受生物过程启发的人工智能系统实现提供基础。

Abstract: This article presents a mathematical model of the Motivated Emotional Mind cognitive architecture developed for embodied intelligent systems. Such a system learns to maintain its homeostasis through a generalized form of reinforcement learning based on its internal motivations, termed motivated learning (ML). The principal contribution of this article is a rigorous formalization of the re-entrant loop integrating feedforward processing, lateral interactions, and feedback pathways, together with the representational selection mechanisms that govern adaptive system responses. The model specifies how ongoing exteroceptive and interoceptive signals, bodily-motivational context, and memory traces are bound into associative memory structures termed semblions, which compete for access to further processing and top-down reconstruction. The formalization encompasses secondary perception, representational competition, curiosity, procedural gaps, and action selection directed toward limiting allostatic violations. Within this framework, motivated learning is tailored to embodied systems whose dynamics are shaped by needs, affect, and the current regulatory state. Unlike standard reinforcement-learning models, the proposed approach incorporates need thresholds, goal generation and shifting goals, bodily state, resource constraints, and action uncertainty, thereby providing a more adequate account of response selection under regulatory pressure. Global affect functions as a central control signal, modulating the learning rate, representational valence, and the balance between exploration and exploitation. The model presented here is a step toward a more rigorous formalization of cognitive phenomena and may provide a basis for further theoretical analysis, computer simulation, and implementation in artificial-intelligence systems inspired by biological processes.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Position Paper: Neurotransmitters as a Missing Dimension in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20083)
*Yupei Li,Manuel Milling,Berrak Sisman,Björn Schuller*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文，主张将神经调质（神经递质）机制作为学习的"第三轴"显式建模进人工神经网络，以赋予其生物系统般的学习灵活性与长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络作为现代深度学习的核心，缺乏生物系统所具有的自适应灵活性与长期稳定性。其根源在于传统ANN依赖统一、局部、基于梯度的参数更新，忽视了神经递质信号与神经可塑性等生物内部学习原理。现有方法多聚焦于架构扩展或正则化、参数隔离等数学层面的微调，未能触及生物学习的本质机制。

Method: 该文为立场论文（positional paper），采用论证与倡议的研究范式而非实验方法。作者类比哺乳动物大脑优越的自适应性与可塑性，提出"神经调质构成学习的第三轴"这一理论主张——即神经调质与神经活动、突触可塑性并列互补；并系统论述将神经调质原理融入ANN设计的合理性，呼吁将这一视角作为自适应与持续学习系统开发的重要方向。

Result: 作为立场论文，本文未报告实验数据或性能指标，其"结果"体现为提出的核心论点：神经调质应被视为区别于神经活动与突触可塑性的学习第三轴，且应在ANN中显式建模；同时指出该方向为有前景但研究不足的领域。

Conclusion: 将神经调质原理纳入ANN设计是一条具有前景且尚未充分探索的研究路径。作者倡导在自适应与持续学习系统的开发中，对这一生物启发视角给予更多关注，以弥补当前ANN在灵活性与稳定性上的不足。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (ANNs), as core components of modern deep learning (DL) systems, lack the adaptive flexibility and long-term stability exhibited by biological systems. This limitation largely stems from the fact that conventional ANNs rely on uniform, local, and gradient-based parameter updates, while neglecting internal learning principles that are biological mechanisms such as neurotransmitters signalling or neuroplasticity. Consequently, many existing approaches focus on architectural expansion or mathematical fine-tuning techniques such as regularisation or parameter isolation. Inspired by the superior adaptability and plasticity of mammalian brains, we posit that neuromodulation with neurotransmitters constitutes a third axis of learning, complementary to neural activity and synaptic plasticity, and should be explicitly modelled in artificial neural networks. In this positional paper, we argue that incorporating neuromodulatory principles into ANN design represents a promising and underexplored research direction, and we advocate for greater attention to this perspective in the development of adaptive and continual learning systems.

</details>
